0.0;\n    T c = 0.0;\n    for (const auto& val : data) {\n        T y = val - c;\n        T t = sum + y;\n        c = (t - sum) - y;\n        sum = t;\n    }\n    return sum;\n}\n\n// Print results                         const size_t mc, const size_t kc) noexcept {\n    for (size_t j0 = 0; j0 < N; j0 += BJ) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(BJ, N - j0);\n        const size_t nFull = nc - nc Blocking over k merely splits that ordered\n// sum into consecutive segments, so the sequence of floating-point operations\n// per element is identical to the sequential version.\n//\n// Structure:\n//   * B is packed once (in parallel) into column panels of NR contiguous\n//     doubles per k. This gives unit-stride, aliasing-free access to B in the\n//     micro-kernel (the natural row-major stride is a multiple of 4 KiB for\n//     power-of-two N, which thrashes L1 sets).\n//   * C is partitioned into 2D tiles (bi x bj) that are distributed over the\n//     threads. The tile size adapts to N and the thread count so there are\n//     enough tiles to balance load even for small matrices.\n//   * Each tile is processed in k-blocks of BK so the A block and B panels\n//     stay resident in L1/L2.\n//   * The micro-kernel computes an MR x NR tile of C with the accumulators\n//     held in SIMD registers (MR*NR/4 256-bit vectors) using GCC/Clang\n//     vector extensions (no external dependencies).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t VL = 4;            // doubles per 256-bit vector\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;            // rows of C per micro-kernel\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;            // columns of C per micro-kernel (2 vectors)\nconstexpr size_t NV = NR / VL;      // vectors per row\nconstexpr size_t BK = 256;          // k extent of a cache block\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BI = 96;       // max rows per tile (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BJ = 256;      // max cols per tile (multiple of NR)\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(VL * sizeof(double))));\n\ninline v4d loadv(const double* p) noexcept {\n    v4d v;\n    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\ninline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) noexcept { __builtin_memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\n\n// C[0..R)[0..ncols) += A[0..R)[0..kc) * Bp[0..kc)[0..NR)\n//   a   : rows of A, stride lda, pointing at column k0\n//   bp  : packed B panel, kc consecutive groups of NR doubles\n//   c   : C tile, stride ldc\n//   ncols: valid columns (<= NR); columns beyond ncols are not stored\ntemplate <size_t R>\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict a, const size_t lda,\n                        const double* __restrict bp,\n                        double* __restrict c, const size_t ldc,\n                        const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    v4d acc[R][NV];\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(c + r * ldc + v * VL);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR] = {};\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) tmp[j] = c[r * ldc + j];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(tmp + v * VL);\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b[NV];\n        for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) b[v] = loadv(bp + k * NR + v * VL);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double s = a[r * lda + k];\n            const v4d av = {s, s, s, s};\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] += av * b[v];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(c + r * ldc + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(tmp + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) c[r * ldc + j] = tmp[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline void runKernel(const size_t rows, const double* a, const size_t lda,\n                      const double* bp, double* c, const size_t ldc,\n                      const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    switch (rows) {\n        case 6: microKernel<6>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 5: microKernel<5>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 4: microKernel<4>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 3: microKernel<3>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 2: microKernel<2>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 1: microKernel<1>(a, lda, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        default: break;\n    }\n}\n\n// Choose tile dimensions so that there are enough tiles to keep all threads\n// busy, while keeping tiles as large as possible for cache reuse.\ninline void chooseTileSize(const size_t N, const size_t nthreads, size_t& bi, size_t& bj) noexcept {\n    bi = MAX_BI;\n    bj = MAX_BJ;\n    const size_t target = 3 * nthreads;\n    auto tiles = [&]() { return ((N + bi - 1) / bi) * ((N + bj - 1) / bj); };\n    while (tiles() < target) {\n        if (bj > 2 * NR && bj >= bi) {\n            bj = (bj / 2 + NR - 1) / NR * NR;\n        } else if (bi > 2 * MR) {\n            bi = (bi / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR;\n        } else if (bj > NR) {\n            bj = (bj / 2 + NR - 1) / NR * NR;\n        } else {\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    const double* __restrict a = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict b = B.data();\n    double* __restrict c = C.data();\n\n    const size_t numPanels = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n    // Packed B: [panel][k][NR]; the last panel is zero padded.\n    std::vector<double> packedB(numPanels * N * NR);\n    double* __restrict bp = packedB.data();\n\n    size_t bi = MAX_BI, bj = MAX_BJ;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n#pragma omp single\n        chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads()), bi, bj);\n        // (implicit barrier after single: bi/bj visible to all threads)\n\n        // Pack B into column panels. Work is split over (panel, k-block) pairs.\n        const size_t numKBlocks = (N + BK - 1) / BK;\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n        for (size_t p = 0; p < numPanels; ++p) {\n            for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numKBlocks; ++kb) {\n                const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n                const size_t nc = std::min(NR, N - j0);\n                const size_t k0 = kb * BK;\n                const size_t k1 = std::min(N, k0 + BK);\n                double* dst = bp + (p * N + k0) * NR;\n                for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k, dst += NR) {\n                    const double* src = b + k * N + j0;\n                    size_t j = 0;\n                    for (; j < nc; ++j) dst[j] = src[j];\n                    for (; j < NR; ++j) dst[j] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // (implicit barrier after the packing loop)\n\n        const size_t numRowTiles = (N + bi - 1) / bi;\n        const size_t numColTiles = (N + bj - 1) / bj;\n\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(dynamic, 1)\n        for (size_t ti = 0; ti < numRowTiles; ++ti) {\n            for (size_t tj = 0; tj < numColTiles; ++tj) {\n                const size_t i0 = ti * bi;\n                const size_t mc = std::min(bi, N - i0);\n                const size_t j0 = tj * bj;\n                const size_t nc = std::min(bj, N - j0);\n                const size_t p0 = j0 / NR;\n                const size_t np = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n                // Start from 0.0 exactly like the reference \"double sum = 0.0\".\n                for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + mc; ++i)\n                    for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + nc; ++j) c[i * N + j] = 0.0;\n\n                for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n                    const size_t kc = std::min(BK, N - k0);\n                    for (size_t p = 0; p < np; ++p) {\n                        const size_t jj = j0 + p * NR;\n                        const size_t ncols = std::min(NR, N - jj);\n                        const double* panel = bp + ((p0 + p) * N + k0) * NR;\n                        for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + mc; i += MR) {\n                            const size_t rows = std::min(MR, i0 + mc - i);\n                            runKernel(rows, a + i * N + k0, N, panel, c + i * N + jj, N, kc, ncols);\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:start] + new_mm + src[end:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncd /tmp/mm_build && make 2>&1 | grep -E \"warning|error|Built\" && for n in 1 7 65 100 512 513 1000; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5-1","id":"msg_011Cg6PzxkrCmJg6K17KgNUg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01JFx7pyVgo6Gy1pJwLEJVms","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/llmtest/evals/20261011-092100/matmul_claude-fable-5.1-cc-medium_omp_r2/matmul && python3 - <<'EOF'\nsrc=open('matmul.cpp').read()\nstart = src.index('// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n// OpenMP matrix multiplication')\nend = src.index('// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare')\nnew_mm = r'''// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// OpenMP matrix multiplication\n//\n// Semantics: every C[i][j] is computed as  ((0.0 + A[i][0]*B[0][j]) + A[i][1]*B[1][j]) + ...\n// i.e. the k-loop is always traversed in increasing order, from 0.0, exactly\n// as in the reference triple loop. Blocking over k merely splits that ordered\n// sum into consecutive segments, so the sequence of floating-point operations\n// per element is identical to the sequential version.\n//\n// Structure (Goto/BLIS style, with OpenMP over 2D tiles of C):\n//   * B is packed once (in parallel) into column panels of NR contiguous\n//     doubles per k. This gives unit-stride, aliasing-free access to B in the\n//     micro-kernel (the natural row-major stride is a multiple of 4 KiB for\n//     power-of-two N, which thrashes L1 sets).\n//   * C is partitioned into 2D tiles (bi x bj) that are distributed statically\n//     over the threads in column-major tile order, so that threads that are\n//     neighbours (same L3) work on the same B panel at the same time. The tile\n//     size is chosen from N and the thread count with a small cost model that\n//     balances load balance against memory traffic (which is ~ 1/bi + 1/bj).\n//   * Each tile is processed in k-blocks of BK. The A block (bi x BK) is packed\n//     into a thread-private buffer in MR-row panels so that both operands are\n//     read with unit stride from L1/L2 inside the micro-kernel.\n//   * The micro-kernel computes an MR x NR tile of C with the accumulators\n//     held in SIMD registers (MR*NR/4 256-bit vectors) using GCC/Clang\n//     vector extensions (no external dependencies).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t VL = 4;            // doubles per 256-bit vector\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;            // rows of C per micro-kernel\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;            // columns of C per micro-kernel (2 vectors)\nconstexpr size_t NV = NR / VL;      // vectors per row\nconstexpr size_t BK = 256;          // k extent of a cache block\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BI = 192;      // max rows per tile (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BJ = 256;      // max cols per tile (multiple of NR)\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(VL * sizeof(double))));\n\ninline v4d loadv(const double* p) noexcept {\n    v4d v;\n    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\ninline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) noexcept { __builtin_memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\n\n// C[0..R)[0..ncols) += Ap[0..kc)[0..R) * Bp[0..kc)[0..NR)\n//   ap  : packed A panel, kc consecutive groups of MR doubles (rows 0..R-1 used)\n//   bp  : packed B panel, kc consecutive groups of NR doubles\n//   c   : C tile, stride ldc\n//   ncols: valid columns (<= NR); columns beyond ncols are not stored\ntemplate <size_t R>\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict ap, const double* __restrict bp,\n                        double* __restrict c, const size_t ldc,\n                        const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    v4d acc[R][NV];\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(c + r * ldc + v * VL);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR] = {};\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) tmp[j] = c[r * ldc + j];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(tmp + v * VL);\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b[NV];\n        for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) b[v] = loadv(bp + k * NR + v * VL);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double s = ap[k * MR + r];\n            const v4d av = {s, s, s, s};\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] += av * b[v];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(c + r * ldc + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(tmp + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) c[r * ldc + j] = tmp[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline void runKernel(const size_t rows, const double* ap, const double* bp,\n                      double* c, const size_t ldc, const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    switch (rows) {\n        case 6: microKernel<6>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 5: microKernel<5>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 4: microKernel<4>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 3: microKernel<3>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 2: microKernel<2>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 1: microKernel<1>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        default: break;\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack A[0..mc)[0..kc) (row stride lda) into MR-row panels: [mc/MR][kc][MR].\n// Rows beyond mc in the last panel are zero filled (never read into C).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict a, const size_t lda, double* __restrict ap,\n                  const size_t mc, const size_t kc) noexcept {\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < mc; i0 += MR) {\n        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i0);\n        double* dst = ap + i0 * kc;\n        if (rows == MR) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR)\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n        } else {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR) {\n                size_t r = 0;\n                for (; r < rows; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n                for (; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Choose the tile size (bi x bj) for a given N and thread count.\n// Cost model per thread: rounds * (compute + memory traffic), where\n// rounds = ceil(tiles / nthreads), compute ~ bi*bj and traffic ~ (bi + bj)\n// scaled by an empirical flops-per-byte balance factor.\ninline void chooseTileSize(const size_t N, const size_t nthreads, size_t& bi, size_t& bj) noexcept {\n    constexpr double kTraffic = 48.0;\n    double bestCost = -1.0;\n    bi = MAX_BI;\n    bj = MAX_BJ;\n    for (size_t cbi = MAX_BI; cbi >= MR; cbi = (cbi > MR) ? std::max(MR, (cbi / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR) : 0) {\n        for (size_t cbj = MAX_BJ; cbj >= NR; cbj /= 2) {\n            const size_t tiles = ((N + cbi - 1) / cbi) * ((N + cbj - 1) / cbj);\n            const size_t rounds = (tiles + nthreads - 1) / nthreads;\n            const double cost = static_cast<double>(rounds) *\n                (static_cast<double>(cbi) * static_cast<double>(cbj) +\n                 kTraffic * static_cast<double>(cbi + cbj));\n            if (bestCost < 0.0 || cost < bestCost) {\n                bestCost = cost;\n                bi = cbi;\n                bj = cbj;\n            }\n            if (cbj == NR) break;\n        }\n        if (cbi == MR) break;\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    const double* __restrict a = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict b = B.data();\n    double* __restrict c = C.data();\n\n    const size_t numPanels = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n    // Packed B: [panel][k][NR]; the last panel is zero padded.\n    // Deliberately not value-initialised: the pages are first touched by the\n    // parallel packing loop (fast, and NUMA-local to the packing thread).\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> packedB(new double[numPanels * N * NR]);\n    double* __restrict bp = packedB.get();\n\n    size_t bi = MAX_BI, bj = MAX_BJ;\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n#pragma omp single\n        chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads()), bi, bj);\n        // (implicit barrier after single: bi/bj visible to all threads)\n\n        // Pack B into column panels. Work is split over (panel, k-block) pairs.\n        const size_t numKBlocks = (N + BK - 1) / BK;\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n        for (size_t p = 0; p < numPanels; ++p) {\n            for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numKBlocks; ++kb) {\n                const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n                const size_t nc = std::min(NR, N - j0);\n                const size_t k0 = kb * BK;\n                const size_t k1 = std::min(N, k0 + BK);\n                double* dst = bp + (p * N + k0) * NR;\n                for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k, dst += NR) {\n                    const double* src = b + k * N + j0;\n                    size_t j = 0;\n                    for (; j < nc; ++j) dst[j] = src[j];\n                    for (; j < NR; ++j) dst[j] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // (implicit barrier after the packing loop)\n\n        // Thread-private packed A block: bi rows (rounded up to MR) x BK.\n        const size_t biPadded = (bi + MR - 1) / MR * MR;\n        std::unique_ptr<double[]> packedA(new double[biPadded * BK]);\n        double* __restrict ap = packedA.get();\n\n        const size_t numRowTiles = (N + bi - 1) / bi;\n        const size_t numColTiles = (N + bj - 1) / bj;\n        const size_t numTiles = numRowTiles * numColTiles;\n\n        // Column-major tile order + static schedule: consecutive threads work\n        // on the same B panel (shared through L3) and on different A blocks.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTiles; ++t) {\n            const size_t ti = t % numRowTiles;\n            const size_t tj = t / numRowTiles;\n            const size_t i0 = ti * bi;\n            const size_t mc = std::min(bi, N - i0);\n            const size_t j0 = tj * bj;\n            const size_t nc = std::min(bj, N - j0);\n            const size_t p0 = j0 / NR;\n            const size_t np = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n            // Start from 0.0 exactly like the reference \"double sum = 0.0\".\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + mc; ++i)\n                for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + nc; ++j) c[i * N + j] = 0.0;\n\n            for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n                const size_t kc = std::min(BK, N - k0);\n                packA(a + i0 * N + k0, N, ap, mc, kc);\n                for (size_t p = 0; p < np; ++p) {\n                    const size_t jj = j0 + p * NR;\n                    const size_t ncols = std::min(NR, N - jj);\n                    const double* panel = bp + ((p0 + p) * N + k0) * NR;\n                    for (size_t i = 0; i < mc; i += MR) {\n                        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i);\n                        runKernel(rows, ap + i * kc, panel, c + (i0 + i) * N + jj, N, kc, ncols);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n'''\nsrc = src[:start] + new_mm + src[end:]\nopen('matmul.cpp','w').write(src)\nEOF\ncat > /tmp/mm_build/mkinstr.py <<'EOF'\nsrc=open('/home/llmtest/evals/20261011-092100/matmul_claude-fable-5.1-cc-medium_omp_r2/matmul/matmul.cpp').read()\nsrc=src.replace('#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"','#include \"/home/llmtest/evals/20261011-092100/matmul_claude-fable-5.1-cc-medium_omp_r2/common/results_output.hpp\"')\nsrc=src.replace('#pragma omp parallel\\n    {\\n#pragma omp single\\n        chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads()), bi, bj);',\n '    double T0=omp_get_wtime(),T1=0,T2=0;\\n#pragma omp parallel\\n    {\\n#pragma omp single\\n        { chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads()), bi, bj);\\n          if(getenv(\"MM_BI\")) bi=atoi(getenv(\"MM_BI\")); Blocking over k merely splits that ordered\n// sum into consecutive segments, so the sequence of floating-point operations\n// per element is identical to the sequential version.\n//\n// Structure (Goto/BLIS style, with OpenMP over 2D tiles of C):\n//   * B is packed once (in parallel) into column panels of NR contiguous\n//     doubles per k. This gives unit-stride, aliasing-free access to B in the\n//     micro-kernel (the natural row-major stride is a multiple of 4 KiB for\n//     power-of-two N, which thrashes L1 sets).\n//   * C is partitioned into 2D tiles (bi x bj) that are distributed statically\n//     over the threads in column-major tile order, so that threads that are\n//     neighbours (same L3) work on the same B panel at the same time. The tile\n//     size is chosen from N and the thread count with a small cost model that\n//     balances load balance against memory traffic (which is ~ 1/bi + 1/bj).\n//   * Each tile is processed in k-blocks of BK. The A block (bi x BK) is packed\n//     into a thread-private buffer in MR-row panels so that both operands are\n//     read with unit stride from L1/L2 inside the micro-kernel.\n//   * The micro-kernel computes an MR x NR tile of C with the accumulators\n//     held in SIMD registers (MR*NR/4 256-bit vectors) using GCC/Clang\n//     vector extensions (no external dependencies).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t VL = 4;            // doubles per 256-bit vector\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;            // rows of C per micro-kernel\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;            // columns of C per micro-kernel (2 vectors)\nconstexpr size_t NV = NR / VL;      // vectors per row\nconstexpr size_t BK = 256;          // k extent of a cache block\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BI = 192;      // max rows per tile (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BJ = 256;      // max cols per tile (multiple of NR)\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(VL * sizeof(double))));\n\ninline v4d loadv(const double* p) noexcept {\n    v4d v;\n    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\ninline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) noexcept { __builtin_memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\n\n// C[0..R)[0..ncols) += Ap[0..kc)[0..R) * Bp[0..kc)[0..NR)\n//   ap  : packed A panel, kc consecutive groups of MR doubles (rows 0..R-1 used)\n//   bp  : packed B panel, kc consecutive groups of NR doubles\n//   c   : C tile, stride ldc\n//   ncols: valid columns (<= NR); columns beyond ncols are not stored\ntemplate <size_t R>\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict ap, const double* __restrict bp,\n                        double* __restrict c, const size_t ldc,\n                        const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    v4d acc[R][NV];\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(c + r * ldc + v * VL);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR] = {};\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) tmp[j] = c[r * ldc + j];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(tmp + v * VL);\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b[NV];\n        for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) b[v] = loadv(bp + k * NR + v * VL);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double s = ap[k * MR + r];\n            const v4d av = {s, s, s, s};\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] += av * b[v];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(c + r * ldc + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(tmp + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) c[r * ldc + j] = tmp[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline void runKernel(const size_t rows, const double* ap, const double* bp,\n                      double* c, const size_t ldc, const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    switch (rows) {\n        case 6: microKernel<6>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 5: microKernel<5>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 4: microKernel<4>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 3: microKernel<3>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 2: microKernel<2>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 1: microKernel<1>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        default: break;\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack A[0..mc)[0..kc) (row stride lda) into MR-row panels: [mc/MR][kc][MR].\n// Rows beyond mc in the last panel are zero filled (never read into C).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict a, const size_t lda, double* __restrict ap,\n                  const size_t mc, const size_t kc) noexcept {\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < mc; i0 += MR) {\n        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i0);\n        double* dst = ap + i0 * kc;\n        if (rows == MR) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR)\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n        } else {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR) {\n                size_t r = 0;\n                for (; r < rows; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n                for (; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Choose the tile size (bi x bj) for a given N and thread count.\n// Cost model per thread: rounds * (compute + memory traffic), where\n// rounds = ceil(tiles / nthreads), compute ~ bi*bj and traffic ~ (bi + bj)\n// scaled by an empirical flops-per-byte balance factor.\ninline void chooseTileSize(const size_t N, const size_t nthreads, size_t& bi, size_t& bj) noexcept {\n    constexpr double kTraffic = 48.0;\n    double bestCost = -1.0;\n    bi = MAX_BI;\n    bj = MAX_BJ;\n    for (size_t cbi = MAX_BI; cbi >= MR; cbi = (cbi > MR) ? std::max(MR, (cbi / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR) : 0) {\n        for (size_t cbj = MAX_BJ; cbj >= NR; cbj /= 2) {\n            const size_t tiles = ((N + cbi - 1) / cbi) * ((N + cbj - 1) / cbj);\n            const size_t rounds = (tiles + nthreads - 1) / nthreads;\n            const double cost = static_cast<double>(rounds) *\n                (static_cast<double>(cbi) * static_cast<double>(cbj) +\n                 kTraffic * static_cast<double>(cbi + cbj));\n            if (bestCost < 0.0 || cost < bestCost) {\n                bestCost = cost;\n                bi = cbi;\n                bj = cbj;\n            }\n            if (cbj == NR) break;\n        }\n        if (cbi == MR) break;\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    const double* __restrict a = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict b = B.data();\n    double* __restrict c = C.data();\n\n    const size_t numPanels = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n    // Packed B: [panel][k][NR]; the last panel is zero padded.\n    // Deliberately not value-initialised: the pages are first touched by the\n    // parallel packing loop (fast, and NUMA-local to the packing thread).\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> packedB(new double[numPanels * N * NR]);\n    double* __restrict bp = packedB.get();\n\n    // Tile size is a pure performance heuristic, so using the expected team\n    // size here (instead of omp_get_num_threads() inside the region) is safe.\n    size_t bi = MAX_BI, bj = MAX_BJ;\n    chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, omp_get_max_threads())), bi, bj);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Pack B into column panels. Work is split over (panel, k-block) pairs.\n        const size_t numKBlocks = (N + BK - 1) / BK;\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n        for (size_t p = 0; p < numPanels; ++p) {\n            for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numKBlocks; ++kb) {\n                const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n                const size_t nc = std::min(NR, N - j0);\n                const size_t k0 = kb * BK;\n                const size_t k1 = std::min(N, k0 + BK);\n                double* dst = bp + (p * N + k0) * NR;\n                for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k, dst += NR) {\n                    const double* src = b + k * N + j0;\n                    size_t j = 0;\n                    for (; j < nc; ++j) dst[j] = src[j];\n                    for (; j < NR; ++j) dst[j] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // (implicit barrier after the packing loop)\n\n        // Thread-private packed A block (MAX_BI x BK doubles). Thread-local\n        // static storage: no per-thread heap allocation inside the parallel\n        // region (mmap/munmap serialise badly across >100 threads).\n        static thread_local double packedA[MAX_BI * BK] __attribute__((aligned(64)));\n        double* __restrict ap = packedA;\n\n        const size_t numRowTiles = (N + bi - 1) / bi;\n        const size_t numColTiles = (N + bj - 1) / bj;\n        const size_t numTiles = numRowTiles * numColTiles;\n\n        // Column-major tile order + static schedule: consecutive threads work\n        // on the same B panel (shared through L3) and on different A blocks.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTiles; ++t) {\n            const size_t ti = t % numRowTiles;\n            const size_t tj = t / numRowTiles;\n            const size_t i0 = ti * bi;\n            const size_t mc = std::min(bi, N - i0);\n            const size_t j0 = tj * bj;\n            const size_t nc = std::min(bj, N - j0);\n            const size_t p0 = j0 / NR;\n            const size_t np = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n            // Start from 0.0 exactly like the reference \"double sum = 0.0\".\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + mc; ++i)\n                for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + nc; ++j) c[i * N + j] = 0.0;\n\n            for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n                const size_t kc = std::min(BK, N - k0);\n                packA(a + i0 * N + k0, N, ap, mc, kc);\n                for (size_t p = 0; p < np; ++p) {\n                    const size_t jj = j0 + p * NR;\n                    const size_t ncols = std::min(NR, N - jj);\n                    const double* panel = bp + ((p0 + p) * N + k0) * NR;\n                    for (size_t i = 0; i < mc; i += MR) {\n                        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i);\n                        runKernel(rows, ap + i * kc, panel, c + (i0 + i) * N + jj, N, kc, ncols);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Default thread placement.\n//\n// The compute kernel is bound by per-core SIMD throughput and L2 capacity, so\n// the best configuration is one thread per physical core, pinned. If the user\n// has not configured the OpenMP runtime (OMP_NUM_THREADS / OMP_PROC_BIND /\n// OMP_PLACES), we select that configuration ourselves from the CPUs the\n// process is allowed to run on (respecting taskset/cgroups). Any explicit user\n// setting takes precedence and disables this logic.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#ifdef __linux__\n// Reads e.g. \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu5/topology/core_id\"; returns -1 on failure.\nstatic long readCpuTopologyValue(const int cpu, const char* leaf) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, leaf);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) return -1;\n    long v = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &v) != 1) v = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return v;\n}\n\n// One allowed CPU per physical core (falls back to all allowed CPUs if the\n// topology cannot be read). Empty on failure.\nstatic std::vector<int> selectOneCpuPerCore() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return {};\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores;  // (package id, core id)\n    bool topologyOk = true;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n        const long pkg = readCpuTopologyValue(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const long core = readCpuTopologyValue(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) { topologyOk = false; cpus.push_back(cpu); continue; }\n        const std::pair<long, long> key(pkg, core);\n        if (std::find(seenCores.begin(), seenCores.end(), key) != seenCores.end()) continue;\n        seenCores.push_back(key);\n        cpus.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (!topologyOk) {\n        // Mixed/unknown topology: use every allowed CPU instead of guessing.\n        cpus.clear();\n        for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu)\n            if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    return cpus;\n}\n#endif\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\")) return;\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    const std::vector<int> cpus = selectOneCpuPerCore();\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n\n    omp_set_dynamic(0);\n    omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cpus.size()));\n\n    // Bind each thread of the (persistent) OpenMP worker pool to its core.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[tid % cpus.size()], &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS\n    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <memory>\n#include <utility>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#ifdef __linux__\n#include <pthread.h>\n#include <sched.h>\n#endif\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\n// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization\nconstexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {\n    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);\n}\n\nvoid initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {\n    double* const m = mat.data();\n    // Parallel initialization: deterministic (each element depends only on its\n    // indices) and provides NUMA-friendly first-touch placement of the pages.\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            m[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// OpenMP matrix multiplication\n//\n// Semantics: every C[i][j] is computed as  ((0.0 + A[i][0]*B[0][j]) + A[i][1]*B[1][j]) + ...\n// i.e. the k-loop is always traversed in increasing order, from 0.0, exactly\n// as in the reference triple loop. Blocking over k merely splits that ordered\n// sum into consecutive segments, so the sequence of floating-point operations\n// per element is identical to the sequential version.\n//\n// Structure (Goto/BLIS style, with OpenMP over 2D tiles of C):\n//   * B is packed once (in parallel) into column panels of NR contiguous\n//     doubles per k. This gives unit-stride, aliasing-free access to B in the\n//     micro-kernel (the natural row-major stride is a multiple of 4 KiB for\n//     power-of-two N, which thrashes L1 sets).\n//   * C is partitioned into 2D tiles (bi x bj) that are distributed statically\n//     over the threads in column-major tile order, so that threads that are\n//     neighbours (same L3) work on the same B panel at the same time. The tile\n//     size is chosen from N and the thread count with a small cost model that\n//     balances load balance against memory traffic (which is ~ 1/bi + 1/bj).\n//   * Each tile is processed in k-blocks of BK. The A block (bi x BK) is packed\n//     into a thread-private buffer in MR-row panels so that both operands are\n//     read with unit stride from L1/L2 inside the micro-kernel.\n//   * The micro-kernel computes an MR x NR tile of C with the accumulators\n//     held in SIMD registers (MR*NR/4 256-bit vectors) using GCC/Clang\n//     vector extensions (no external dependencies).\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\nnamespace {\n\nconstexpr size_t VL = 4;            // doubles per 256-bit vector\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;            // rows of C per micro-kernel\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;            // columns of C per micro-kernel (2 vectors)\nconstexpr size_t NV = NR / VL;      // vectors per row\nconstexpr size_t BK = 256;          // k extent of a cache block\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BI = 192;      // max rows per tile (multiple of MR)\nconstexpr size_t MAX_BJ = 256;      // max cols per tile (multiple of NR)\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(VL * sizeof(double))));\n\ninline v4d loadv(const double* p) noexcept {\n    v4d v;\n    __builtin_memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(v));\n    return v;\n}\ninline void storev(double* p, const v4d v) noexcept { __builtin_memcpy(p, &v, sizeof(v)); }\n\n// C[0..R)[0..ncols) += Ap[0..kc)[0..R) * Bp[0..kc)[0..NR)\n//   ap  : packed A panel, kc consecutive groups of MR doubles (rows 0..R-1 used)\n//   bp  : packed B panel, kc consecutive groups of NR doubles\n//   c   : C tile, stride ldc\n//   ncols: valid columns (<= NR); columns beyond ncols are not stored\ntemplate <size_t R>\ninline void microKernel(const double* __restrict ap, const double* __restrict bp,\n                        double* __restrict c, const size_t ldc,\n                        const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    v4d acc[R][NV];\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(c + r * ldc + v * VL);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR] = {};\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) tmp[j] = c[r * ldc + j];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] = loadv(tmp + v * VL);\n        }\n    }\n\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        v4d b[NV];\n        for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) b[v] = loadv(bp + k * NR + v * VL);\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            const double s = ap[k * MR + r];\n            const v4d av = {s, s, s, s};\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) acc[r][v] += av * b[v];\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (ncols == NR) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r)\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(c + r * ldc + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < R; ++r) {\n            double tmp[NR];\n            for (size_t v = 0; v < NV; ++v) storev(tmp + v * VL, acc[r][v]);\n            for (size_t j = 0; j < ncols; ++j) c[r * ldc + j] = tmp[j];\n        }\n    }\n}\n\ninline void runKernel(const size_t rows, const double* ap, const double* bp,\n                      double* c, const size_t ldc, const size_t kc, const size_t ncols) noexcept {\n    switch (rows) {\n        case 6: microKernel<6>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 5: microKernel<5>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 4: microKernel<4>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 3: microKernel<3>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 2: microKernel<2>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        case 1: microKernel<1>(ap, bp, c, ldc, kc, ncols); break;\n        default: break;\n    }\n}\n\n// Pack A[0..mc)[0..kc) (row stride lda) into MR-row panels: [mc/MR][kc][MR].\n// Rows beyond mc in the last panel are zero filled (never read into C).\ninline void packA(const double* __restrict a, const size_t lda, double* __restrict ap,\n                  const size_t mc, const size_t kc) noexcept {\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < mc; i0 += MR) {\n        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i0);\n        double* dst = ap + i0 * kc;\n        if (rows == MR) {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR)\n                for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n        } else {\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k, dst += MR) {\n                size_t r = 0;\n                for (; r < rows; ++r) dst[r] = a[(i0 + r) * lda + k];\n                for (; r < MR; ++r) dst[r] = 0.0;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Choose the tile size (bi x bj) for a given N and thread count.\n// Cost model per thread: rounds * (compute + memory traffic), where\n// rounds = ceil(tiles / nthreads), compute ~ bi*bj and traffic ~ (bi + bj)\n// scaled by an empirical flops-per-byte balance factor.\ninline void chooseTileSize(const size_t N, const size_t nthreads, size_t& bi, size_t& bj) noexcept {\n    constexpr double kTraffic = 48.0;\n    double bestCost = -1.0;\n    bi = MAX_BI;\n    bj = MAX_BJ;\n    for (size_t cbi = MAX_BI; cbi >= MR; cbi = (cbi > MR) ? std::max(MR, (cbi / 2 + MR - 1) / MR * MR) : 0) {\n        for (size_t cbj = MAX_BJ; cbj >= NR; cbj /= 2) {\n            const size_t tiles = ((N + cbi - 1) / cbi) * ((N + cbj - 1) / cbj);\n            const size_t rounds = (tiles + nthreads - 1) / nthreads;\n            const double cost = static_cast<double>(rounds) *\n                (static_cast<double>(cbi) * static_cast<double>(cbj) +\n                 kTraffic * static_cast<double>(cbi + cbj));\n            if (bestCost < 0.0 || cost < bestCost) {\n                bestCost = cost;\n                bi = cbi;\n                bj = cbj;\n            }\n            if (cbj == NR) break;\n        }\n        if (cbi == MR) break;\n    }\n}\n\n}  // namespace\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, \n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    if (N == 0) return;\n\n    const double* __restrict a = A.data();\n    const double* __restrict b = B.data();\n    double* __restrict c = C.data();\n\n    const size_t numPanels = (N + NR - 1) / NR;\n    // Packed B: [panel][k][NR]; the last panel is zero padded.\n    // Deliberately not value-initialised: the pages are first touched by the\n    // parallel packing loop (fast, and NUMA-local to the packing thread).\n    std::unique_ptr<double[]> packedB(new double[numPanels * N * NR]);\n    double* __restrict bp = packedB.get();\n\n    // Tile size is a pure performance heuristic, so using the expected team\n    // size here (instead of omp_get_num_threads() inside the region) is safe.\n    size_t bi = MAX_BI, bj = MAX_BJ;\n    chooseTileSize(N, static_cast<size_t>(std::max(1, omp_get_max_threads())), bi, bj);\n\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        // Pack B into column panels. Work is split over (panel, k-block) pairs.\n        const size_t numKBlocks = (N + BK - 1) / BK;\n#pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)\n        for (size_t p = 0; p < numPanels; ++p) {\n            for (size_t kb = 0; kb < numKBlocks; ++kb) {\n                const size_t j0 = p * NR;\n                const size_t nc = std::min(NR, N - j0);\n                const size_t k0 = kb * BK;\n                const size_t k1 = std::min(N, k0 + BK);\n                double* dst = bp + (p * N + k0) * NR;\n                for (size_t k = k0; k < k1; ++k, dst += NR) {\n                    const double* src = b + k * N + j0;\n                    size_t j = 0;\n                    for (; j < nc; ++j) dst[j] = src[j];\n                    for (; j < NR; ++j) dst[j] = 0.0;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        // (implicit barrier after the packing loop)\n\n        // Thread-private packed A block (MAX_BI x BK doubles). Thread-local\n        // static storage: no per-thread heap allocation inside the parallel\n        // region (mmap/munmap serialise badly across >100 threads).\n        static thread_local double packedA[MAX_BI * BK] __attribute__((aligned(64)));\n        double* __restrict ap = packedA;\n\n        const size_t numRowTiles = (N + bi - 1) / bi;\n        const size_t numColTiles = (N + bj - 1) / bj;\n        const size_t numTiles = numRowTiles * numColTiles;\n\n        // Column-major tile order + static schedule: consecutive threads work\n        // on the same B panel (shared through L3) and on different A blocks.\n#pragma omp for schedule(static)\n        for (size_t t = 0; t < numTiles; ++t) {\n            const size_t ti = t % numRowTiles;\n            const size_t tj = t / numRowTiles;\n            const size_t i0 = ti * bi;\n            const size_t mc = std::min(bi, N - i0);\n            const size_t j0 = tj * bj;\n            const size_t nc = std::min(bj, N - j0);\n            const size_t p0 = j0 / NR;\n            const size_t np = (nc + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n            // Start from 0.0 exactly like the reference \"double sum = 0.0\".\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < i0 + mc; ++i)\n                for (size_t j = j0; j < j0 + nc; ++j) c[i * N + j] = 0.0;\n\n            for (size_t k0 = 0; k0 < N; k0 += BK) {\n                const size_t kc = std::min(BK, N - k0);\n                packA(a + i0 * N + k0, N, ap, mc, kc);\n                for (size_t p = 0; p < np; ++p) {\n                    const size_t jj = j0 + p * NR;\n                    const size_t ncols = std::min(NR, N - jj);\n                    const double* panel = bp + ((p0 + p) * N + k0) * NR;\n                    for (size_t i = 0; i < mc; i += MR) {\n                        const size_t rows = std::min(MR, mc - i);\n                        runKernel(rows, ap + i * kc, panel, c + (i0 + i) * N + jj, N, kc, ncols);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Default thread placement.\n//\n// The compute kernel is bound by per-core SIMD throughput and L2 capacity, so\n// the best configuration is one thread per physical core, pinned. If the user\n// has not configured the OpenMP runtime (OMP_NUM_THREADS / OMP_PROC_BIND /\n// OMP_PLACES), we select that configuration ourselves from the CPUs the\n// process is allowed to run on (respecting taskset/cgroups). Any explicit user\n// setting takes precedence and disables this logic.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#ifdef __linux__\n// Reads e.g. \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu5/topology/core_id\"; returns -1 on failure.\nstatic long readCpuTopologyValue(const int cpu, const char* leaf) {\n    char path[128];\n    snprintf(path, sizeof(path), \"/sys/devices/system/cpu/cpu%d/topology/%s\", cpu, leaf);\n    FILE* f = fopen(path, \"r\");\n    if (!f) return -1;\n    long v = -1;\n    if (fscanf(f, \"%ld\", &v) != 1) v = -1;\n    fclose(f);\n    return v;\n}\n\n// One allowed CPU per physical core (falls back to all allowed CPUs if the\n// topology cannot be read). Empty on failure.\nstatic std::vector<int> selectOneCpuPerCore() {\n    cpu_set_t allowed;\n    CPU_ZERO(&allowed);\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(allowed), &allowed) != 0) return {};\n\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    std::vector<std::pair<long, long>> seenCores;  // (package id, core id)\n    bool topologyOk = true;\n    for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu) {\n        if (!CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) continue;\n        const long pkg = readCpuTopologyValue(cpu, \"physical_package_id\");\n        const long core = readCpuTopologyValue(cpu, \"core_id\");\n        if (pkg < 0 || core < 0) { topologyOk = false; cpus.push_back(cpu); continue; }\n        const std::pair<long, long> key(pkg, core);\n        if (std::find(seenCores.begin(), seenCores.end(), key) != seenCores.end()) continue;\n        seenCores.push_back(key);\n        cpus.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    if (!topologyOk) {\n        // Mixed/unknown topology: use every allowed CPU instead of guessing.\n        cpus.clear();\n        for (int cpu = 0; cpu < CPU_SETSIZE; ++cpu)\n            if (CPU_ISSET(cpu, &allowed)) cpus.push_back(cpu);\n    }\n    return cpus;\n}\n#endif\n\nstatic void configureThreads() {\n#ifdef __linux__\n    if (getenv(\"OMP_NUM_THREADS\") || getenv(\"OMP_PROC_BIND\") || getenv(\"OMP_PLACES\")) return;\n    if (omp_get_proc_bind() != omp_proc_bind_false) return;\n\n    const std::vector<int> cpus = selectOneCpuPerCore();\n    if (cpus.empty()) return;\n\n    omp_set_dynamic(0);\n    omp_set_num_threads(static_cast<int>(cpus.size()));\n\n    // Bind each thread of the (persistent) OpenMP worker pool to its core.\n#pragma omp parallel\n    {\n        const size_t tid = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        cpu_set_t one;\n        CPU_ZERO(&one);\n        CPU_SET(cpus[tid % cpus.size()], &one);\n        pthread_setaffinity_np(pthread_self(), sizeof(one), &one);\n    }\n#endif\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            N = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    configureThreads();\n\n    printf(\"Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Matrix size: %zu x %zu\\n\", N, N);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", omp_get_max_threads());\n    \n    // Allocate matrices\n    std::vector<double> A(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(N * N);\n    \n    // Initialize matrices\n    printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    initMatrix(A, N);\n    initMatrix(B, N);\n    \n    // Perform matrix multiplication\n    printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Calculate GFLOPS\n    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;\n    printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        print_results(C, \"MatrixC\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation\n    if (validate) {\n The matmul benchmark is now OpenMP-parallel, builds cleanly, and is verified against the original.\n\n**What changed** (only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt`, executable name unchanged):\n\n- **Kernel rewrite.** The triple loop became a Goto/BLIS-style blocked multiply. B is packed once in parallel into 8-column panels, each thread packs its A block into 6-row panels, and a 6x8 register-tiled micro-kernel (GCC/Clang vector extensions, no intrinsics headers or libraries) does the work. I verified in the assembly that the accumulators stay in registers with zero spills.\n- **Parallel decomposition.** C is split into 2D tiles distributed with a static schedule in column-major order, so neighbouring cores reuse the same B panel from shared L3. Tile sizes come from a small cost model that weighs load balance against memory traffic for the given N and thread count. Matrix initialization is also parallel, which gives NUMA-local first touch.\n- **Default thread placement.** With no OMP environment set, the program uses one pinned thread per physical core taken from the process's allowed CPU mask (taskset and cgroups respected). Any of OMP_NUM_THREADS, OMP_PROC_BIND, or OMP_PLACES disables this. On this box the runtime otherwise launches 256 unpinned SMT threads, where a single barrier costs 5 ms and siblings thrash L2, roughly halving throughput.\n- **CMake.** Added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. OpenMP is mandatory, not optional.\n\n**Correctness.** Every C element is accumulated over k in increasing order from 0.0, exactly as the original loop, so the floating-point operation sequence is identical. With FMA contraction disabled on both builds, the result hashes match the original bit-for-bit for 19 sizes from 1 to 1024, including non-multiples of the tile sizes. With the default build flags there are last-bit differences at some odd sizes, caused solely by where GCC chose to fuse multiply-adds in the original's loop tails. Validation passes everywhere.\n\n**Performance** (EPYC 7763, 128 cores, default environment, median of 3):\n\n| N | Original | New | Speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 512 | 723 ms | 4 ms | 180x |\n| 1024 | 7968 ms | 8 ms | 1000x |\n| 2048 | 99088 ms | 19 ms | 5200x |\n| 4096 | ~13 min (est.) | 92 ms | ~8500x |\n\nSingle-thread throughput alone rose from 0.37 to about 25 GFLOPS. At N=4096 the parallel run reaches 1.5 TFLOPS, about 58x the single-thread rate on 128 cores. The remaining gap to linear scaling comes from all-core AVX frequency reduction and shared-cache bandwidth. If you'd rather leave placement entirely to the OpenMP runtime, delete the `configureThreads` call in main and the two helper functions above it. The matmul benchmark is now OpenMP-parallel, builds cleanly, and is verified against the original.\n\n**What changed** (only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt`, executable name unchanged):\n\n- **Kernel rewrite.** The triple loop became a Goto/BLIS-style blocked multiply. B is packed once in parallel into 8-column panels, each thread packs its A block into 6-row panels, and a 6x8 register-tiled micro-kernel (GCC/Clang vector extensions, no intrinsics headers or libraries) does the work. I verified in the assembly that the accumulators stay in registers with zero spills.\n- **Parallel decomposition.** C is split into 2D tiles distributed with a static schedule in column-major order, so neighbouring cores reuse the same B panel from shared L3. Tile sizes come from a small cost model that weighs load balance against memory traffic for the given N and thread count. Matrix initialization is also parallel, which gives NUMA-local first touch.\n- **Default thread placement.** With no OMP environment set, the program uses one pinned thread per physical core taken from the process's allowed CPU mask (taskset and cgroups respected). Any of OMP_NUM_THREADS, OMP_PROC_BIND, or OMP_PLACES disables this. On this box the runtime otherwise launches 256 unpinned SMT threads, where a single barrier costs 5 ms and siblings thrash L2, roughly halving throughput.\n- **CMake.** Added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. OpenMP is mandatory, not optional.\n\n**Correctness.** Every C element is accumulated over k in increasing order from 0.0, exactly as the original loop, so the floating-point operation sequence is identical. With FMA contraction disabled on both builds, the result hashes match the original bit-for-bit for 19 sizes from 1 to 1024, including non-multiples of the tile sizes.
